# Market-condition analysis (baseline trades, scenario B)

Tags known before the trade: trend = 63-day return of the previous close divided by its volatility scale (strong > 1, weak 0.3-1, sideways |z| < 0.3); volatility = 20-day realised volatility of the previous close ranked in the instrument's own history (low < p25, normal, high > p75, extreme > p95); session = XM server hour of entry (Asia 0-7, London 8-12, London/NY 13-16, New York 17-21, Sydney 22-23); weekend gap = Monday open vs Friday close in ATR(14) units. A 'failed breakout' is a losing trade whose best excursion never reached 0.3 R.
